# Strategy Validation Matrix

Scope: all implemented TTrades strategies, all available assets and reasonable timeframes.

Data cap per dataset for this pass: 40000 bars.

Mode: FULL

Optimization: if naive (no fee/slippage) is not tradable, realism sweeps are skipped.

Units: crypto net is `%`; futures net is `$` per 1 micro contract (MNQ=$2/pt, MES=$5/pt, GOLD=$10/pt).
